# Prediabetes Pal GDM Door — Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

> **Read me first — the rule that shapes this plan: the plan may not open a gate.** Code may be written and merged behind the flag. Nothing user-visible ships before its sign-off. S1–S5 and the three claim classes are **rows in §1 with a decider who is not the executor** — never tasks. An executor who finds a gate closed stops at the merge; an executor never edits a `Status` cell in `docs/safety/copy-ledger.md` to `Approved`, never edits a `Status` cell in `docs/safety/gdm-gates.md`, and never edits a file on the never-edited list (Global Constraints). The production build enforces all three mechanically (Task 0.2).

> **Reviewed 2026-09-20 by `/autoplan`; approved by the owner with tiered amendments.** The record is at the end of this file (`# /autoplan review record`), decisions `G-01` … `G-62`. How to read the marks:
> - **`(G-nn)` inside a step** = the step was rewritten by the review. Eight critical and high findings are applied this way: G-09, G-10, G-12, G-27, G-30, G-32, G-44, G-52.
> - **A "Review amendments" note under a task heading** = read it before starting the task. Each line names the change in a few words; the reasoning and the evidence are in the record under the same id. These are requirements, not suggestions.
> - **Owner decisions at the review gates:** premises P1 and P2 are owner rows in §1 (G-33) · the server twin **fails closed** (G-09, option A) · the appointment block stays on Home in every state (G-32) · the maker is named once, in the GDM footer (G-48) · `organiser` stays one surface (G-01b) · the nav arrangement in G-34 · no separate DX pass (G-61).
> - **One correction to the rule above (G-31):** the production build enforces what a `Status` cell *says*. It cannot know who typed it. The other half of that control is the owner reading the diff, and Task 0.2 now pins the four gate statuses in a test so that signing a gate is a two-file change nobody can miss.

**Goal:** Build the GDM door — a separate front door on the same app for a person told she has gestational diabetes and waiting for her dietitian — as flagged, PR-sized phases: Tier 1's general organiser now (questions, her plan in her clinician's words, waiting mode, her own meals, a one-page summary), and Tier 2's food features specified so that the safety owner's sign-off is the only thing between each and a build.

**Architecture:** One surface-listed build flag (`NEXT_PUBLIC_GDM_DOOR`: `landing`, `organiser`, `ideas`, `read`) with a server twin (`GDM_DOOR_ENABLED`) gates everything and is §9.2's revert lever; a production guard refuses any surface whose ledger rows are not `Approved` or whose gates are not `Signed`. The door lives under `app/gdm/**`, **outside** the `(app)` shell (the `app/guides/layout.tsx` standalone pattern), so it has no tab bar, no `FirstRunGate`, no link to `/check`, and edits no prediabetes-door file. Health data lives in two new consent-gated, field-encrypted tables (`gdm_profiles`, `gdm_items`) behind `/api/gdm/*` routes that never import `lib/pal/a1c.ts`. Every product-authored string lives in one bounded bank (`lib/pal/gdm/copy.ts`) that is tested for ledger parity, so the ledger and the source cannot drift. Four deep modules are pure and testable alone: the plan record, the summary builder (Tier 1), the question router and the plan read (Tier 2).

**Tech Stack:** Next 16.3 App Router (read `node_modules/next/dist/docs/01-app/` before touching an API), React 19.2, TypeScript 6, zod 4, drizzle-orm + Postgres (PGlite in tests via `tests/helpers/test-db.ts`), vitest 4 (`environment: "node"`, no jsdom), Playwright smoke under `tests/smoke/`, Umami analytics via `lib/client/analytics.ts`. **Tier 1 adds no dependency.**

**Spec:** `PRD/Prediabetes_Pal_GDM_Door_PRD_v1.1.md` (wins on any conflict). Superseded, never planned from: `PRD/Prediabetes_Pal_GDM_Door_PRD_v1.md`. Rationale only: `docs/handoff/2026-09-12-gestational-top5-pains-outcomes.md`. Governance: `docs/safety/claims-boundary.md`, `docs/safety/copy-ledger.md`, `DESIGN.md`, `CLAUDE.md`. House format: `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-13-guide-redesign.md`.

## Global Constraints

- **The plan may not open a gate** (above). Merge ≠ release. Every gate's decider is named in §1.
- **Never edited by any task:** `docs/safety/claims-boundary.md` · `lib/pal/clinical-risk.ts` (the `pregnancy` route and the four routes above it) · `lib/pal/boundary-copy.ts` · `DESIGN.md` · `docs/ICP.md` · both GDM PRDs · the prediabetes door (`app/page.tsx`, everything under `app/(app)/`, `app/welcome/`, `components/` outside `components/gdm/`, `lib/pal/labels.ts`, `lib/pal/service.ts`, `lib/pal/prompt.ts`, `lib/pal/a1c.ts`) · `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-13-guide-redesign.md`. `lib/pal/*` engine behaviour stays pinned by `tests/unit/pal/engine-regression.test.ts` — never edit those expectations. Shared infrastructure this plan *does* extend, additively: `lib/server/db/schema.ts`, `next.config.ts`, `app/api/health/route.ts`, `app/api/account/health-data/route.ts`, `app/api/account/export/route.ts`, `lib/client/analytics.ts`, `app/robots.ts`, `app/globals.css`, `scripts/*`, `.github/workflows/ci.yml`, docs under `docs/ops/`, `docs/privacy/`, and the ledger; and, **in Tier 2 only**, `proxy.ts` and `lib/pal/rate-limit.ts` (one metered route, Task G3.3) and `lib/pal/openai-client.ts` (**export-only**, no behaviour change, Task G3.2 — the one touch on an engine file, flagged for the owner in §8 item 12).
- **The clinical line does not move** (PRD header): "no product sentence about a reading, a dose, a delivery decision, the pregnancy or the baby." Where a pain is clinical the product organises, parks, cites and stays calm. It never rules.
- **Two strings are banned from every GDM surface: `safe` and `for your baby`**, in either direction (PRD §8 rule 1). In this plan they appear only where a rule or a test defines them.
- **No number the user did not type** (PRD §8 rule 5): no carbohydrate figure, target, range, default or estimate. No bank string contains a digit. **No unit conversion, anywhere on the door.**
- **Bounded banks, never free generation**, for every product-authored string (PRD §7.4). All GDM copy lives in `lib/pal/gdm/copy.ts` (Tier 1) and the Tier 2 banks named in §5. **Components carry no literal copy** — they import from the bank; Task 0.4's test enforces it.
- **Every new string is a copy-ledger row** in `docs/safety/copy-ledger.md`, shipped `Pending | Yes` behind the flag (the `photo-draft-*` / `guide-ideas-*` precedent), claim class per PRD §8.1 (`gdm-organiser` for all of Tier 1). `npm run contract` checks the claim class **only on `Approved` rows** (`scripts/validate-safety-contract.mjs` `checkClaimsBoundary`), so a `Pending` row naming a class that does not exist yet passes — and the row cannot become `Approved` until the safety owner adds the class. The ledger parser splits cells on `|`: separate several strings in one Copy cell with ` · `.
- **Under-eating is the named hazard** (PRD §8 rule 4). Every bank is positive-only; nothing is framed as a reduction; no streaks, scores or badges; no countdown framed as pressure (F-CALM-GDM).
- **Health data:** the GDM profile flag and everything under it is health data (PRD §7.4). Explicit consent before any write ("A signed-in session is not consent" — `app/api/check/route.ts:488-496`), every free-text or date field through `encryptField` (`lib/server/crypto.ts`), every new table has `userId` → `users.id` with `onDelete: "cascade"`, explicit coverage in the health-data erase transaction and the account export. No column may be literally named `a1c` or `food` (`tests/unit/pal/privacy-stateful.test.ts:41-49`).
- **A1C-free and medication-free:** nothing under `app/gdm/`, `app/api/gdm/`, `components/gdm/` or `lib/pal/gdm/` imports `lib/pal/a1c.ts`, `lib/pal/labels.ts`, `lib/pal/service.ts` or `lib/pal/boundary-copy.ts`. No A1C is asked. Medication is never asked and **no field for it exists** (D2).
- **The `pregnancy` route stays on everywhere.** Scoping it off for the GDM profile is S2 — the safety owner's, not this plan's. Tier 2's read box calls `classifyClinicalRisk` and honours **every** route, `pregnancy` included, until S2 is signed.
- **Analytics** (`lib/client/analytics.ts`): closed enums only, never a bare `string` prop, never `umami.identify`. The no-PII source scan (`tests/unit/client/analytics.test.ts`) fails on the identifiers `a1c`, `food`, `email`, `reason`, `question`, `message`, `disclaimer`, `swap`, `adjustment`, `examples`, `sequencingTip`, `postMealAction` **anywhere in the file, comments included** — so GDM events say `ask` and `meal`, never the banned words. Insert every new union member **before** the `photo_draft` variant. Never free text, plan contents, meal text, or the profile flag joined to an identity.
- **Flag convention:** `NEXT_PUBLIC_GDM_DOOR` is surface-listed (`1` = every surface, dev/preview/e2e only; production always carries the explicit list). Because the door adds server routes that store health data, it gets the **full twin pair** — the repo's own rule (`app/api/health/route.ts:191-193`: a flag may skip the twin only when "the door adds no server boundary"). Every `/api/gdm/*` route 404s unless `GDM_DOOR_ENABLED === "1"`. Every new env var needs a row in `docs/ops/env-reference.md` (`tests/unit/env-contract.test.ts`).
- **Storage keys** use the `pal.gdm.*` prefix (`pal.gdm.ask.draft`, `pal.gdm.ideas.rotation`, `pal.gdm.ideas.pref`). Every accessor is try/catch-wrapped (the `lib/client/orientation-store.ts` pattern). Nothing health-related is *stored* on-device; on-device keys hold an unsent draft or a rotation counter only.
- **New unit tests go under `tests/unit/pal/`** (file prefix `gdm-`), PGlite route tests included, so `npm run test:pal` runs the whole door. Existing tests are extended in place (`tests/unit/client/analytics.test.ts`, `tests/unit/server/health.test.ts`, `health-data-delete.test.ts`, `account-export.test.ts`, `db-schema.test.ts`, `tests/unit/pal/flag-server-twins.test.ts`, `tests/unit/next-config-twin-guard.test.ts`, `tests/unit/pal/seo-meta.test.ts`, `tests/unit/pal/claims-boundary-copy.test.ts` `EXTRA_SOURCES`). Smoke specs under `tests/smoke/`.
- **Keep the protected `revora` strings** listed in `CLAUDE.md` (owned-domains denylist, `sw-dev-teardown` hosts, the docstrings in `lib/pal/contact.ts` and `lib/server/email.ts`, historical docs). Do not "clean them up".
- **Design rails** (`DESIGN.md` §1): one accent `#0d5f57`; 44px targets; icons only from `components/icons.tsx`; radius scale 24/22/18/14/999; no dark bands; WCAG AA, health information never in `--text-soft`; rail 16 — every sentence fileable under a claim class. One global sheet (`app/globals.css`; the repo has no CSS modules). F-SUMMARY uses **browser print** (`window.print()` + the existing `@media print` block at `app/globals.css:3898`), not a PDF library. Dates use native `<input type="date">`.
- **Next 16 facts this plan relies on** (verified in `node_modules/next/dist/docs/01-app/`): the request interceptor is `proxy.ts`, not `middleware.ts` (`03-api-reference/03-file-conventions/proxy.md`); `params` and `searchParams` are **Promises** (`page.md`), typed with the global `PageProps<'/route'>` / `RouteContext<'/route'>` helpers that `next typegen` generates (the `typecheck` script runs it); `notFound()` throws and must be called in the render path (`04-functions/not-found.md`); **auth checks belong in each page through a data-access helper, not in a layout** — layouts do not re-render on navigation (`02-guides/authentication.md` "Layouts and auth checks"). `cacheComponents` is not enabled in `next.config.ts`. Re-read the guide before using any API not named here.
- **Every commit:** `npm run lint && npm run typecheck && npm run test:pal && npm run contract`. Commits are the executor's, at execution time — this planning session commits nothing.
- **Evidence standing** (PRD §1.5): the twelve pains are a set, not a sequence; nothing depends on rank. Every threshold in this plan is **[A]** — chosen without a baseline.

## 2. Tier 1 phases (PR-sized, in PRD §9 Step 2 order)

### 2.1 Order, and what each PR waits on

| PR | Phase | Feature(s) | Tasks | Merges when | Releases when |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| PR-0 | Foundations — no user-visible string | Flag pair, production guard + gates doc, ops wiring, the one GDM copy test, the `pregnancy`-route pin, analytics events | 0.1–0.6 | Ready | Never on its own — it renders nothing |
| PR-L | Landing route with a sign-up. **Built second so it can release first** if `gdm-organiser` clears its line (PRD §9 Step 2) | §3 landing, §7.1 | L.1–L.2 | Ready | `gdm-organiser` exists **and** `landing`'s rows are `Approved` ⇒ `NEXT_PUBLIC_GDM_DOOR=landing` |
| PR-1 | Door shell | Health-data consent, A1C-free onboarding (§7.1, §7.2), erase + export, Your data, the privacy notice | 1.1–1.5 | **Only after** migration `0020` is applied to production and verified (G-10, Task 1.1). This is the one dark PR that is not safe to merge early | With `organiser` |
| PR-2 | F-ASKLIST | Questions for my appointment | 2.1–2.3 | Ready | With `organiser` |
| PR-3 | F-PLANKEEP | My plan, in my clinician's words | 3.1–3.4 | Ready | With `organiser` |
| PR-4 | F-WAIT | Waiting-for-my-dietitian mode | 4.1–4.3 | Ready | With `organiser` |
| PR-5 | F-MYMEALS | My own meal list | 5.1–5.2 | Ready | With `organiser` |
| PR-6 | F-SUMMARY + the kill-line reads | One page for the appointment; `launch-controls.md` §14 | 6.1–6.4 | Ready | With `organiser`: batch G1 `Approved`, the privacy notice read by counsel, `GDM_DOOR_ENABLED=1` |

**F-CALM-GDM is not a PR.** It is the last task of every phase (Tasks L.2, 1.5, 2.3, 3.4, 4.3, 5.2, 6.4): the copy test green over the phase's new rows, plus the audit checklist in §7.1.

`organiser` is one surface because PRD §9 Step 2 asks for "one ledger batch for all Tier 1 strings": the features merge one by one, dark, and open together. If the owner wants F-ASKLIST live ahead of the rest (PRD §11.1 calls it "the cheapest live signal"), split `organiser` into per-feature surfaces then — a ten-line change to `lib/gdm-door-flag.ts`, deliberately not built now.

### 2.3 Tasks

#### PR-0 — Foundations

##### Task 0.0: A clean place to start (G-52)

On 2026-09-20 this plan and the PRD it implements were both **untracked**, and the working tree held 60 untracked files (51 under `docs/`) and six modified ones, `docs/safety/copy-ledger.md` and `DESIGN.md` among them. An executor in a fresh worktree would not find the plan. An executor in this tree would commit the owner's unrelated work.

- [ ] **Step 1 (owner):** commit or stash the six modified files. `DESIGN.md` is never-edited by this plan: do not "fix" its diff.
- [ ] **Step 2:** on a docs branch, commit exactly three files and merge them, as the guide plan was:

```bash
git switch -c docs/gdm-door-plan

- [ ] **Step 3:** start PR-0 from a clean worktree off `main` (`superpowers:using-git-worktrees`). `git status --short` prints nothing before Task 0.1 begins.

**Two rules for every task below.**

1. **Every `git add` names files or the door's own directories** (`app/gdm`, `app/api/gdm`, `components/gdm`, `lib/pal/gdm`). Never `git add lib`, `tests` or `docs`. Where a commit block in this plan still lists a broad directory, narrow it.
2. **A narrow test run names the file (G-49).** `npm run test:pal -- gdm-door` does not run one file: `test:pal` is `vitest run tests/unit/pal`, the appended word is a second filter, and vitest runs a file that matches *either*. Measured: 74 files with the word, 74 without. Wherever a step below says `npm run test:pal -- <name> …`, run `npx vitest run tests/unit/pal/<name>.test.ts …` instead. Keep plain `npm run test:pal` for the end-of-task and every-commit runs.

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the flag module**

```ts
// lib/gdm-door-flag.ts
/**
 * The GDM-door flag (PRD/Prediabetes_Pal_GDM_Door_PRD_v1.1.md §9 Step 2). One
 * env var gates every surface of the second front door and is §9.2's revert
 * lever. Same surface-listed shape as lib/guide-door-flag.ts: `1` opens every
 * surface (dev, previews, the e2e build); a comma list opens only those named;
 * anything else opens none. Production never carries `1`.
 *
 * Unlike the guide door this one has a SERVER TWIN. The door adds API routes
 * that store health data, and the repo's rule (app/api/health/route.ts) is
 * that a client flag may skip the twin only when it adds no server boundary.
 * Every /api/gdm/* route 404s unless GDM_DOOR_ENABLED is exactly "1".
 *
 * THE TWIN IS THE INCIDENT LEVER, AND IT FAILS CLOSED (review G-09). On Vercel
 * an env change only takes effect through a redeploy, and a redeploy runs the
 * production guard. So the guard must never refuse a missing twin: it CLOSES
 * every surface instead (lib/gdm-door-guard.ts). Unset either variable and
 * redeploy, and the door is dark. This differs on purpose from the four
 * `twinMismatch` pairs in next.config.ts, which throw.
 *
 * Surfaces map to the PRD's gates, not to features:
 *   landing   — the public route + sign-up            (gdm-organiser)
 *   organiser — all of Tier 1, one ledger batch       (gdm-organiser)
 *   ideas     — F-IDEAS-GDM                           (S1–S3, gdm-food-ideas)
 *   read      — F-ROUTER, the read box, F-PLANREAD    (S1–S4, gdm-plan-read)
 */
export const GDM_SURFACES = ["landing", "organiser", "ideas", "read"] as const;
export type GdmSurface = (typeof GDM_SURFACES)[number];

/**
 * Every copy-ledger Copy ID each surface renders. The production guard
 * (lib/gdm-door-guard.ts) refuses a surface unless every row listed here is
 * `Approved` and `Active = Yes`, and refuses a surface with no rows at all.
 * Concrete IDs only. The task that files a row adds it here.
 */
export const GDM_SURFACE_ROWS: Record<GdmSurface, readonly string[]> = {
  landing: [],
  organiser: [],
  ideas: [],
  read: []
};

export const GDM_SURFACE_REQUIRES: Partial<Record<GdmSurface, readonly GdmSurface[]>> = {
  ideas: ["organiser"],
  read: ["organiser"]
};

/** The safety owner's non-copy gates (PRD §10.2), recorded in docs/safety/gdm-gates.md. */
export const GDM_GATES = ["S1", "S2", "S3", "S4"] as const;
export type GdmGate = (typeof GDM_GATES)[number];

export const GDM_SURFACE_GATES: Record<GdmSurface, readonly GdmGate[]> = {
  landing: [],
  organiser: [],
  ideas: ["S1", "S2", "S3"],
  read: ["S1", "S2", "S3", "S4"]
};

export function gdmDoorEnabled(surface: GdmSurface): boolean {
  const value = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_GDM_DOOR ?? "";
  if (value === "1") return true;
  return value
    .split(",")
    .map((token) => token.trim())
    .includes(surface);
}

export function gdmDoorServerEnabled(
  env: { GDM_DOOR_ENABLED?: string } = process.env as unknown as {
    GDM_DOOR_ENABLED?: string;
  }
): boolean {
  return env.GDM_DOOR_ENABLED === "1";
}
```

Add to the flag table in `docs/ops/env-reference.md`, after the `NEXT_PUBLIC_GUIDE_DOOR` / `PAL_E2E_GUIDE_DOOR` rows:

```
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_GDM_DOOR` | GDM door (PRD GDM v1.1 §9) | **GDM-door build flag** (`lib/gdm-door-flag.ts` `gdmDoorEnabled`): `1` opens every surface; a comma list of surfaces (`landing`, `organiser`, `ideas`, `read`) opens only those; anything else opens none; production never carries `1`. Unset ⇒ every `/gdm/*` route 404s and nothing about the prediabetes door changes. **Has a server twin** (`GDM_DOOR_ENABLED`) because the door stores health data. **Enforced at build** when `VERCEL_ENV=production` (`lib/gdm-door-guard.ts`): a twin that is not `1` **closes every surface and the build still succeeds** (fail closed, review G-09); with the twin on, every ledger row a listed surface renders must be `Approved` and `Active`, and the gates the surface needs (`docs/safety/gdm-gates.md`: S1–S3 for `ideas`, S1–S4 for `read`) must read `Signed`. §9.2 kill lines and D7's spending trigger are read from `docs/ops/launch-controls.md` §14; a fired line ⇒ unset this. |
| `GDM_DOOR_ENABLED` | GDM door (PRD GDM v1.1 §9) | **GDM-door server twin / runtime kill switch** (`gdmDoorServerEnabled`): only exact `1` serves `/api/gdm/*` and the signed-in `/gdm/*` pages — anything else 404s them. **This is the incident lever: unset it and redeploy.** The production guard treats a missing twin as a closed door and never fails that build (review G-09), so this one variable darkens every GDM surface, the landing included. (On Vercel a redeploy *is* a rebuild; there is no env flip without one.) Stored data stays erasable and exportable with the door closed (`/api/account/health-data`, `/api/account/export` do not read this flag). |
```

**Interfaces:**

- Produces: `checkProductionGdmDoor(value, serverTwin, ledgerText, gatesText, rows?): { effective: string; errors: string[] }`, `gateStatuses(gatesText): Map<string, string>`, `GDM_GATES_PATH`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Create the gates record**

```markdown
<!-- docs/safety/gdm-gates.md -->
# GDM door — safety-owner gates

The production build reads the table below (`lib/gdm-door-guard.ts`). A food
surface of the GDM door cannot be listed in `NEXT_PUBLIC_GDM_DOOR` in production
until every gate it needs reads `Signed`.

**Only the decider named on a row edits that row's `Status`.** An implementer
never does — not to unblock a build, not on a preview, not "temporarily".
`Signed` means the decider has done the thing in PRD
`PRD/Prediabetes_Pal_GDM_Door_PRD_v1.1.md` §10.2 and recorded where.

| Gate | What it is | Decider | Status | Signed by | Date | Evidence |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `S1` | Intended use extended to a GDM profile; Verdict Semantics entry for the three word-states | Safety owner (counsel) | Not started | — | — | — |
| `S2` | `pregnancy` route scoped off for the GDM profile only | Safety owner | Not started | — | — | — |
| `S3` | A reviewer with pregnancy competence (RDN/CDCES) named | Safety owner | Not started | — | — | — |
| `S4` | Ruling on "lighter than your plan says" for readers who may inject insulin, and its precedence under `possible_hypoglycemia` | Safety owner | Not started | — | — | — |

S5 (the three claim classes) has no row here on purpose: a class is enforced
through the ledger — `npm run contract` refuses an `Approved` row whose class is
not in `docs/safety/claims-boundary.md`.
```

```ts
// tests/unit/pal/gdm-door-guard.test.ts
import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

import { checkProductionGdmDoor, gateStatuses } from "../../../lib/gdm-door-guard";

const HEADER =
  "| Copy ID | Surface | Status | Active | Allowed Claim Class | Copy | Evidence Rows | Notes |\n" +
  "| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |\n";
const row = (id: string, status: string, active = "Yes") =>
  `| \`${id}\` | GDM | ${status} | ${active} | \`gdm-organiser\` | x | — | — |\n`;
const gates = (s: Record<string, string>) =>
  "| Gate | What it is | Decider | Status |\n|---|---|---|---|\n" +
  Object.entries(s).map(([gate, status]) => `| \`${gate}\` | x | Safety owner | ${status} |\n`).join("");

const ROWS = { landing: ["gdm-a"], organiser: ["gdm-b"], ideas: ["gdm-c"], read: ["gdm-d"] } as const;
const ALL_APPROVED = HEADER + ["gdm-a", "gdm-b", "gdm-c", "gdm-d"].map((id) => row(id, "Approved")).join("");
const NONE_SIGNED = gates({ S1: "Not started", S2: "Not started", S3: "Not started", S4: "Not started" });
const ALL_SIGNED = gates({ S1: "Signed", S2: "Signed", S3: "Signed", S4: "Signed" });

describe("checkProductionGdmDoor — no gate opens by accident", () => {
  it("unset is fine and opens nothing", () => {
    expect(checkProductionGdmDoor(undefined, undefined, "", "", ROWS)).toEqual({ effective: "", errors: [] });
  });

  it("refuses `1`, an unknown token, and a food surface without the organiser", () => {
    expect(checkProductionGdmDoor("1", "1", ALL_APPROVED, ALL_SIGNED, ROWS).errors[0]).toMatch(/"1" in production/);
    expect(checkProductionGdmDoor("organizer", "1", ALL_APPROVED, ALL_SIGNED, ROWS).errors[0]).toMatch(/unknown surface/);
    expect(checkProductionGdmDoor("ideas", "1", ALL_APPROVED, ALL_SIGNED, ROWS).errors.join()).toMatch(/requires "organiser"/);
  });

  // G-09: the revert. Unsetting the twin alone must yield a build that LOADS
  // with the door dark. If this threw, the redeploy would fail and the previous
  // deployment, door open, would keep serving through the incident.
  it("the server twin fails closed: not `1` closes every surface and is never an error", () => {
    for (const value of ["landing", "landing,organiser", "organiser,ideas,read", "1", "nonsense"]) {
      for (const twin of [undefined, "", "0", "true"]) {
        const check = checkProductionGdmDoor(value, twin, ALL_APPROVED, NONE_SIGNED, ROWS);
        expect(check.effective, `${value} / ${twin}`).toBe("");
        expect(check.errors, `${value} / ${twin}`).toEqual([]);
        expect(check.warnings?.join()).toMatch(/GDM_DOOR_ENABLED.*CLOSED/);
      }
    }
  });

  // G-31: signing a gate is the decider's act. Pinning the real file makes it a
  // two-file change, so no reviewer can miss a flipped Status cell.
  it("the real gates record reads exactly as its deciders left it", () => {
    const text = fs.readFileSync(path.join(process.cwd(), "docs/safety/gdm-gates.md"), "utf8");
    expect(Object.fromEntries(gateStatuses(text))).toEqual({
      S1: "Not started",
      S2: "Not started",
      S3: "Not started",
      S4: "Not started"
    });
  });

  // G-60: the person who edits that file is not a developer.
  it.each(["signed", "Signed ✅", "Signed 2026-10-01"])("a near-miss %j is refused, and the error says what the cell must read", (cell) => {
    const almost = gates({ S1: cell, S2: "Signed", S3: "Signed", S4: "Signed" });
    expect(checkProductionGdmDoor("organiser,ideas", "1", ALL_APPROVED, almost, ROWS).errors.join()).toMatch(
      /must read exactly "Signed"/
    );
  });

  it("no `gdm-organiser` class ⇒ nothing ships: a Pending, missing or retired row refuses its surface", () => {
    const pending = HEADER + row("gdm-a", "Pending");
    expect(checkProductionGdmDoor("landing", "1", pending, NONE_SIGNED, ROWS).errors.join()).toMatch(/gdm-a.*Pending/);
    expect(checkProductionGdmDoor("landing", "1", HEADER, NONE_SIGNED, ROWS).errors.join()).toMatch(/not in the copy ledger/);
    const retired = HEADER + row("gdm-a", "Approved", "No");
    expect(checkProductionGdmDoor("landing", "1", retired, NONE_SIGNED, ROWS).errors.join()).toMatch(/Active is No/);
  });

  it("a surface with no rows filed yet is refused", () => {
    const check = checkProductionGdmDoor("landing", "1", ALL_APPROVED, NONE_SIGNED, { ...ROWS, landing: [] });
    expect(check.errors.join()).toMatch(/no ledger rows yet/);
  });

  it("Tier 1 needs no S-gate; it opens on Approved rows alone", () => {
    expect(checkProductionGdmDoor("landing,organiser", "1", ALL_APPROVED, NONE_SIGNED, ROWS)).toEqual({
      effective: "landing,organiser",
      errors: []
    });
  });

  it("no S1–S3 ⇒ no food content; no S4 ⇒ no read — even with every row Approved", () => {
    expect(checkProductionGdmDoor("organiser,ideas", "1", ALL_APPROVED, NONE_SIGNED, ROWS).errors.join()).toMatch(
      /"ideas" needs gate S1.*Not started/
    );
    const s4Open = gates({ S1: "Signed", S2: "Signed", S3: "Signed", S4: "Not started" });
    expect(checkProductionGdmDoor("organiser,ideas", "1", ALL_APPROVED, s4Open, ROWS).errors).toEqual([]);
    expect(checkProductionGdmDoor("organiser,read", "1", ALL_APPROVED, s4Open, ROWS).errors.join()).toMatch(
      /"read" needs gate S4/
    );
    expect(checkProductionGdmDoor("organiser,ideas,read", "1", ALL_APPROVED, ALL_SIGNED, ROWS).errors).toEqual([]);
  });

  it("a missing or unreadable gates file fails closed", () => {
    expect(checkProductionGdmDoor("organiser,ideas", "1", ALL_APPROVED, "", ROWS).errors.join()).toMatch(/gate S1/);
    expect(gateStatuses("no table here").size).toBe(0);
  });
});

- [ ] **Step 4: Write the guard**

```ts
// lib/gdm-door-guard.ts
import { ledgerStatuses } from "./guide-door-guard";
import {
  GDM_SURFACES,
  GDM_SURFACE_GATES,
  GDM_SURFACE_REQUIRES,
  GDM_SURFACE_ROWS,
  type GdmSurface
} from "./gdm-door-flag";

/**
 * The GDM door's production guard. Pure: no fs, no env — next.config.ts
 * (production builds only) and scripts/check-production-config.ts read the
 * ledger and the gates record off disk and pass them in.
 *
 * It is what makes PRD §9.2's first kill line mechanical: "No gdm-organiser
 * class → nothing ships. No S1–S3 → no food content ships. No S4 → no read
 * ships." A row can only be Approved once its class exists
 * (scripts/validate-safety-contract.mjs), and a gate only reads Signed when
 * its decider wrote it (docs/safety/gdm-gates.md).
 *
 * Every error names its fix. `effective` is "" whenever there is an error.
 */
export const GDM_GATES_PATH = "docs/safety/gdm-gates.md";

/** `warnings` is present only when there is one, so `toEqual({ effective, errors })` stays exact elsewhere. */
export type GdmDoorCheck = { effective: string; errors: string[]; warnings?: string[] };

export function checkProductionGdmDoor(
  value: string | undefined,
  serverTwin: string | undefined,
  ledgerText: string,
  gatesText: string,
  rows: Record<GdmSurface, readonly string[]> = GDM_SURFACE_ROWS
): GdmDoorCheck {
  const raw = (value ?? "").trim();
  if (raw === "") return { effective: "", errors: [] };
  // G-09 — FAIL CLOSED, and first. The twin is the incident lever, so pulling it
  // alone has to work: a twin that is not "1" closes every surface and is never
  // an error, whatever else the value says. A guard that threw here would fail
  // the very redeploy that carries the revert, and the old deployment would
  // keep serving. The warning is for the first release, where a forgotten twin
  // now means a dark door and a build-log line instead of a failed build.
  if (serverTwin !== "1") {
    return {
      effective: "",
      errors: [],
      warnings: [
        "NEXT_PUBLIC_GDM_DOOR lists surfaces but GDM_DOOR_ENABLED is not 1 — every GDM surface is CLOSED in this build. " +
          "Set GDM_DOOR_ENABLED=1 to open them."
      ]
    };
  }
  if (raw === "1") {
    return {
      effective: "",
      errors: ['NEXT_PUBLIC_GDM_DOOR is "1" in production — list the surfaces whose rows are Approved instead']
    };
  }

  const errors: string[] = [];
  const surfaces: GdmSurface[] = [];
  for (const token of [...new Set(raw.split(",").map((t) => t.trim()).filter(Boolean))]) {
    if ((GDM_SURFACES as readonly string[]).includes(token)) surfaces.push(token as GdmSurface);
    else errors.push(`unknown surface "${token}" — one of: ${GDM_SURFACES.join(", ")}`);
  }

  const listed = new Set<GdmSurface>(surfaces);
  const ledger = ledgerStatuses(ledgerText);
  const gateStatus = gateStatuses(gatesText);

  for (const surface of surfaces) {
    const missing = (GDM_SURFACE_REQUIRES[surface] ?? []).filter((required) => !listed.has(required));
    if (missing.length > 0) {
      errors.push(`surface "${surface}" requires ${missing.map((m) => `"${m}"`).join(", ")} — add it or remove "${surface}"`);
    }

    for (const gate of GDM_SURFACE_GATES[surface]) {
      const status = gateStatus.get(gate) ?? "missing";
      if (status !== "Signed") {
        errors.push(
          `surface "${surface}" needs gate ${gate}, which reads "${status}" in ${GDM_GATES_PATH} ` +
            `(the cell must read exactly "Signed") — only its decider signs it; remove "${surface}" until then`
        );
      }
    }

    if (rows[surface].length === 0) {
      errors.push(`surface "${surface}" has no ledger rows yet — remove it`);
      continue;
    }
    for (const id of rows[surface]) {
      const found = ledger.get(id);
      if (!found) {
        errors.push(`surface "${surface}" renders row "${id}" which is not in the copy ledger — file it, get it Approved, or remove "${surface}"`);
        continue;
      }
      const notApproved = found.find((state) => state.status !== "Approved");
      if (notApproved) {
        errors.push(`surface "${surface}" renders row "${id}" whose Status is ${notApproved.status || "empty"} — get it Approved or remove "${surface}"`);
        continue;
      }
      const retired = found.find((state) => state.active !== "Yes");
      if (retired) {
        errors.push(`surface "${surface}" renders row "${id}" whose Active is ${retired.active || "empty"} (retired) — reactivate the row or remove "${surface}"`);
      }
    }
  }

  return { effective: errors.length > 0 ? "" : surfaces.join(","), errors };
}

/** Gate id → Status cell, from the table in docs/safety/gdm-gates.md whose header names `Gate` and `Status`. */
export function gateStatuses(gatesText: string): Map<string, string> {
  const statuses = new Map<string, string>();
  let columns: { gate: number; status: number } | null = null;
  for (const line of gatesText.split(/\r?\n/)) {
    const trimmed = line.trim();
    if (!trimmed.startsWith("|")) {
      columns = null;
      continue;
    }
    const cells = trimmed
      .replace(/^\|/, "")
      .replace(/\|$/, "")
      .split("|")
      .map((cell) => cell.trim().replace(/^`(.+)`$/, "$1"));
    if (!columns) {
      const gate = cells.indexOf("Gate");
      const status = cells.indexOf("Status");
      if (gate !== -1 && status !== -1) columns = { gate, status };
      continue;
    }
    if (/^:?-{3,}:?$/.test(cells[0] ?? "")) continue;
    if (cells[columns.gate]) statuses.set(cells[columns.gate], cells[columns.status] ?? "");
  }
  return statuses;
}
```

```ts
// GDM door guard (PRD GDM v1.1 §9.2): never `1`, server twin set, every row a
// listed surface renders Approved, every gate it needs Signed — or the build
// throws. Same waiver posture as the guards above: PAL_ALLOW_NO_MEASUREMENT
// never waives it.
const gdmDoorEnv: { NEXT_PUBLIC_GDM_DOOR?: string } = {};
if (process.env.VERCEL_ENV === "production") {
  const gdmDoor = checkProductionGdmDoor(
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_GDM_DOOR,
    process.env.GDM_DOOR_ENABLED,
    readIfPresent(COPY_LEDGER_PATH) ?? "",
    readIfPresent(GDM_GATES_PATH) ?? ""
  );
  if (gdmDoor.errors.length > 0) {
    throw new Error(
      "NEXT_PUBLIC_GDM_DOOR refused by the production door guard:\n- " + gdmDoor.errors.join("\n- ")
    );
  }
  // G-09: a missing twin is a closed door, not a failed build. Say so in the log.
  for (const warning of gdmDoor.warnings ?? []) console.warn(`[gdm-door] ${warning}`);
  gdmDoorEnv.NEXT_PUBLIC_GDM_DOOR = gdmDoor.effective;
}

The twin lives **inside** the guard (not in the `twinMismatch` array at `:51-65`, whose entries test `=== "1"` and cannot express a surface list). **It also behaves differently from those four pairs, on purpose (G-09):** they *throw* when the twin is unset, this one *closes the door*. Because the effective value is inlined as `""`, a missing twin darkens the landing as well as the organiser, and `/api/health` reports every surface `off`, which is what the door then is.

##### Task 0.4: One copy test over every GDM bank

> **Review amendment (G-17).** Replace the last case's two regexes with a walk of the TypeScript AST (`typescript` is already a dependency; about twenty-five lines). As written, `/(?<!=)>\s*[A-Za-z][^<>{}]{2,}</` reads `days > limit && <Row />` as JSX text, a false alarm the first time a component compares two identifiers, and it cannot see `{"Saving"}`, `{saving ? "Saving" : label}`, `aria-label={'Close'}` or a template literal, which is how a literal really arrives. Flag a non-blank `JsxText`, and any string literal inside a `JsxExpression` or a `JsxAttribute` whose name is not on this allowlist: `className`, `href`, `id`, `type`, `role`, `htmlFor`, `inputMode`, `accept`, `capture`, `name`, `rel`, `target`, `key`, `autoComplete`. Keep a `KNOWN_BAD` control for each of the four bypasses and one `KNOWN_GOOD` for the comparison. A guard that cries wolf is a guard somebody loosens on a deadline.
> **Review note (G-64).** A green `npm run contract` proves nothing about a `Pending` row's claim class: `checkClaimsBoundary` looks only at rows that are `Approved` **and** `Active = Yes`. Every GDM row will pass while naming a class that does not exist. The first thing that checks it is the production guard, on the day someone tries to flip the flag.

**Files:**
- Create: `lib/pal/gdm/copy.ts`
- Modify: `tests/unit/pal/claims-boundary-copy.test.ts` — add `"lib/pal/gdm/copy.ts"` to `EXTRA_SOURCES` (`:315-342`). **The `BANNED` families are not touched.**
- Test: `tests/unit/pal/gdm-copy.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces: `GDM_COPY: Record<CopyId, Record<name, string>>` (Copy ID → that row's named strings, declaration order = ledger order); the test's `GDM_BANKS` list, which Tier 2 tasks extend with their banks.

Pattern: `tests/unit/pal/claims-boundary-copy.test.ts` (families + a `KNOWN_BAD` control per family) and `clinical-copy-no-treatment.test.ts`. What this test adds that neither has: **ledger parity** — `DESIGN.md` §1.1's named gap ("Nothing connects the ledger to the source in either direction") is closed for this door.

```ts
// tests/unit/pal/gdm-copy.test.ts
import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

import { GDM_SURFACE_ROWS } from "../../../lib/gdm-door-flag";
import { GDM_COPY } from "../../../lib/pal/gdm/copy";
import { getCopyLedgerRows } from "../../../scripts/validate-safety-contract.mjs";

const ROOT = process.cwd();

/** A bank is Copy ID → named strings; the ledger sees them in declaration order. */
const toRows = (bank: Record<string, Record<string, string>>): Record<string, readonly string[]> =>
  Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(bank).map(([id, row]) => [id, Object.values(row)]));

/** Every bank of product-authored GDM strings. Tier 2 tasks append theirs (G1.1, G2.1, G3.2). */
const GDM_BANKS: Array<{ name: string; rows: Record<string, readonly string[]> }> = [
  { name: "lib/pal/gdm/copy.ts", rows: toRows(GDM_COPY) }
];

type Family = { label: string; pattern: RegExp; exemptRows?: string[] };

// PRD §8 rule 1, F-CALM-GDM (§6.2), §8 rules 4–5, §7.2. Each family has a control below.
const FAMILIES: Family[] = [
  { label: "banned-string-safe", pattern: /safe/i },
  { label: "banned-string-for-your-baby", pattern: /for\s+your\s+baby/i },
  {
    label: "grading-word",
    pattern:
      /\b(?:good|bad|cheat(?:ed|ing|s)?|fail(?:ed|ing|s|ure)?|slip(?:ped|s)?|earn(?:ed|s)?|deserv(?:e|ed|es)|behav(?:e|ed|es|ing))\b/i
  },
  { label: "blame-grammar", pattern: /\byou\s+(?:went\s+over|overate|ate\s+too\s+much|should(?:\s+not|n't)?\s+have)\b/i },
  { label: "gamification", pattern: /\b(?:streaks?|scores?|badges?|points|level\s+up)\b/i },
  {
    label: "medication-word",
    pattern:
      /\b(?:insulin|metformin|glyburide|medication|medicines?|meds?|dos(?:e|es|age|ing)|units?|inject(?:s|ed|ing|ion|ions)?|pills?|tablets?|prescri\w+)\b/i
  },
  {
    label: "delivery-word",
    pattern: /\b(?:induc(?:e|ed|tion)|c-?section|ca?esarean|deliver(?:y|ed|ing|s)?|labou?r|birth|due\s+date)\b/i
  },
  { label: "digit", pattern: /\d/ },
  {
    label: "reduction",
    pattern:
      /\b(?:eat(?:ing)?\s+less|smaller\s+portions?|cut\s+(?:back|down|out)|reduc(?:e|ed|ing)|restrict\w*|avoid\w*|limit\w*|skip\s+(?:a\s+)?(?:meal|snack|breakfast|lunch|dinner))\b/i
  }
];

const KNOWN_BAD: Record<string, string> = {
  "banned-string-safe": "Ideas that are safe to eat",
  "banned-string-for-your-baby": "A calmer plan for your baby",
  "grading-word": "You had a good day",
  "blame-grammar": "You went over at lunch",
  gamification: "Keep your streak going",
  "medication-word": "Are you taking insulin?",
  "delivery-word": "Questions about your induction",
  digit: "Aim for 45 grams",
  reduction: "Try eating less at dinner"
};

const allStrings = GDM_BANKS.flatMap((bank) =>
  Object.entries(bank.rows).flatMap(([id, strings]) => strings.map((text) => ({ bank: bank.name, id, text })))
);

describe("GDM copy banks — one test over every product-authored string (PRD §9.3)", () => {
  it("has a control sample for every family, and every control trips its family", () => {
    expect(Object.keys(KNOWN_BAD).sort()).toEqual(FAMILIES.map((f) => f.label).sort());
    for (const family of FAMILIES) expect(KNOWN_BAD[family.label]).toMatch(family.pattern);
  });

  it("no bank string trips a family", () => {
    const hits = allStrings.flatMap(({ id, text }) =>
      FAMILIES.filter((f) => !f.exemptRows?.includes(id) && f.pattern.test(text)).map((f) => `${id}: "${text}" → ${f.label}`)
    );
    expect(hits).toEqual([]);
  });

  it("ledger parity: every bank row is a ledger row with the same strings, Active, in a GDM claim class", () => {
    const failures: string[] = [];
    const ledger = getCopyLedgerRows(fs.readFileSync(path.join(ROOT, "docs/safety/copy-ledger.md"), "utf8"), failures);
    expect(failures).toEqual([]);
    for (const bank of GDM_BANKS) {
      for (const [id, strings] of Object.entries(bank.rows)) {
        const row = ledger.find((entry: Record<string, string>) => entry["Copy ID"] === id);
        expect(row, `${id} has no ledger row`).toBeDefined();
        expect(row!.Copy, `${id}: ledger Copy cell differs from the bank`).toBe(strings.join(" · "));
        expect(row!.Active).toBe("Yes");
        expect(["gdm-organiser", "gdm-food-ideas", "gdm-plan-read"]).toContain(row!["Allowed Claim Class"]);
      }
    }
  });

  it("flag parity: the surfaces list exactly the rows the banks hold", () => {
    const inBanks = GDM_BANKS.flatMap((bank) => Object.keys(bank.rows)).sort();
    const inSurfaces = [...new Set(Object.values(GDM_SURFACE_ROWS).flat())].sort();
    expect(inSurfaces).toEqual(inBanks);
  });

  it("GDM components carry no literal copy — every visible string comes from a bank", () => {
    const files = ["app/gdm", "components/gdm"].flatMap((dir) => walk(dir));
    const offenders: string[] = [];
    for (const rel of files) {
      const source = fs
        .readFileSync(path.join(ROOT, rel), "utf8")
        .split("\n")
        .filter((line) => !/^\s*(?:\/\/|\/\*|\*)/.test(line))
        .join("\n");
      // `(?<!=)` keeps an arrow function's `=> a.b < c` from reading as JSX text.
      if (/(?<!=)>\s*[A-Za-z][^<>{}]{2,}</.test(source)) offenders.push(`${rel}: JSX text`);
      if (/\b(?:aria-label|placeholder|title|alt)="[^"]+"/.test(source)) offenders.push(`${rel}: literal attribute`);
    }
    expect(offenders).toEqual([]);
  });
});

function walk(dir: string, out: string[] = []): string[] {
  const abs = path.join(ROOT, dir);
  if (!fs.existsSync(abs)) return out;
  for (const entry of fs.readdirSync(abs, { withFileTypes: true })) {
    const rel = path.join(dir, entry.name);
    if (entry.isDirectory()) walk(rel, out);
    else if (entry.name.endsWith(".tsx")) out.push(rel);
  }
  return out;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Create the bank, empty**

```ts
// lib/pal/gdm/copy.ts
/**
 * The GDM door's Tier 1 copy bank (PRD GDM v1.1 §7.4: "Bounded banks, never
 * free generation, for every product-authored string").
 *
 * Keyed by copy-ledger Copy ID; each row is a record of NAMED strings, so a
 * component reads `GDM_COPY["gdm-door-name"].home`, never an index. The row's
 * strings IN DECLARATION ORDER are the ledger's Copy cell:
 * tests/unit/pal/gdm-copy.test.ts asserts the cell equals
 * `Object.values(row).join(" · ")`, so the ledger and the source cannot drift,
 * and that lib/gdm-door-flag.ts lists exactly these rows. Components import
 * from here and carry no literal copy of their own.
 *
 * Every row ships `Pending | Yes`, class `gdm-organiser`. Rules (PRD §8): no
 * sentence about a reading, a dose, a delivery decision, the pregnancy or the
 * baby; no digit; no grading word; nothing framed as a reduction.
 */
export const GDM_COPY = {} as const satisfies Record<string, Record<string, string>>;
```

Importing `getCopyLedgerRows` from the `.mjs` is an existing pattern: `tests/unit/pal/safety-contract-script.test.ts:16` imports from the same file, and the script guards its module-level `main()`, so the import runs nothing. **Ledger rule that follows from the parity assertion:** a GDM row's Copy cell is plain text — no backticks, no `|`, strings joined by ` · ` — because the parser strips one wrapping pair of backticks from a cell and would mangle a cell that starts and ends with one.

##### Task 0.5: Pin the `pregnancy` route on, everywhere

The plan must not scope the route off — that is S2. This task makes sure nobody does it by accident. It is a characterization test: **it passes on first run**, because it pins behaviour that exists. Its negative controls are what prove the probes discriminate.

**Files:**
- Test: `tests/unit/pal/gdm-pregnancy-route.test.ts` (nothing else is created or modified)

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the test**

```ts
// tests/unit/pal/gdm-pregnancy-route.test.ts
import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

import { CLINICAL_ROUTES, classifyClinicalRisk } from "../../../lib/pal/clinical-risk";
import { checkFood } from "../../../lib/pal/service";

const ROOT = process.cwd();
const throwingModel = {
  generate: () => {
    throw new Error("The model must never be called on a clinical route.");
  }
};

describe("the pregnancy route stays on (PRD §9.3; S2 is the safety owner's, not the plan's)", () => {
  it("the order is the policy: four routes outrank pregnancy, and it is fifth", () => {
    expect(CLINICAL_ROUTES.slice(0, 5)).toEqual([
      "urgent_symptoms",
      "possible_hypoglycemia",
      "medication_dosing",
      "eating_disorder",
      "pregnancy"
    ]);
  });

  // One probe per pattern in ROUTE_PATTERNS.pregnancy — the route stays whole.
  it.each([
    "is sushi ok in pregnancy",
    "im preggers and craving cake",
    "gestational diabetes, is rice ok",
    "i have gdm, is oatmeal ok",
    "we are expecting a baby, is brie ok",
    "im 28 weeks pregnant, pasta for dinner",
    "breastfeeding, is coffee alright",
    "nursing my baby, can i have tuna",
    "trying to conceive, is soy ok"
  ])("fires on: %s", (text) => {
    expect(classifyClinicalRisk(text)?.route).toBe("pregnancy");
  });

  it.each(["pregame snacks and wings", "chicken and rice with broccoli"])("does not fire on: %s", (text) => {
    expect(classifyClinicalRisk(text)).toBeNull();
  });

  it.each([
    ["im pregnant and have been vomiting all morning", "urgent_symptoms"],
    ["gestational diabetes and my blood sugar is 48", "possible_hypoglycemia"],
    ["im pregnant, how much insulin for this pasta", "medication_dosing"],
    ["pregnant and i make myself throw up after meals", "eating_disorder"]
  ] as const)("the four routes above it still win: %s", (text, route) => {
    expect(classifyClinicalRisk(text)?.route).toBe(route);
  });

  it.each([6.0, 5.2, 7.1])("the engine refuses pregnancy input with no model call at A1C %s", async (a1c) => {
    const response = await checkFood({ food: "im 30 weeks pregnant, chicken and rice", a1c }, { model: throwingModel });
    expect(JSON.stringify(response)).toContain("pregnancy");
  });

  it("the engine's one entry point runs the clinical router before anything else", () => {
    const service = fs.readFileSync(path.join(ROOT, "lib/pal/service.ts"), "utf8");
    const router = service.indexOf("classifyClinicalRisk(request.food)");
    expect(router).toBeGreaterThan(-1);
    expect(router).toBeLessThan(service.indexOf("routeA1C(request.a1c)"));
  });

  it("no GDM file reaches the prediabetes engine, its labels, its A1C router or its boundary copy", () => {
    const offenders: string[] = [];
    for (const dir of ["app/gdm", "app/api/gdm", "components/gdm", "lib/pal/gdm"]) {
      for (const rel of walk(dir)) {
        const source = fs.readFileSync(path.join(ROOT, rel), "utf8");
        if (/from\s+["'][^"']*\/pal\/(?:service|labels|a1c|boundary-copy|prompt)["']/.test(source)) offenders.push(rel);
      }
    }
    expect(offenders).toEqual([]);
  });

  it("no GDM file filters a clinical route by name (that would be S2 by the back door)", () => {
    const offenders: string[] = [];
    for (const dir of ["app/gdm", "app/api/gdm", "components/gdm", "lib/pal/gdm"]) {
      for (const rel of walk(dir)) {
        if (/["']pregnancy["']/.test(fs.readFileSync(path.join(ROOT, rel), "utf8"))) offenders.push(rel);
      }
    }
    expect(offenders).toEqual([]);
  });
});

function walk(dir: string, out: string[] = []): string[] {
  const abs = path.join(ROOT, dir);
  if (!fs.existsSync(abs)) return out;
  for (const entry of fs.readdirSync(abs, { withFileTypes: true })) {
    const rel = path.join(dir, entry.name);
    if (entry.isDirectory()) walk(rel, out);
    else if (/\.tsx?$/.test(entry.name)) out.push(rel);
  }
  return out;
}
```

**Interfaces:**
- Produces: `GDM_OCCASIONS`, `type GdmOccasion`, `GDM_UNITS`, `type GdmUnit`; the events below. Every later task calls `track()` with these and adds no event of its own.

| Event | Props | PRD §9.1 measure / kill line |
|---|---|---|
| `gdm_door_shown` | `surface: "landing" \| "home"` | door shown |
| `gdm_onboarding_step` | `step: "told" \| "not_told_exit" \| "date" \| "consent" \| "done"` | row 7: exits at the first step are counted, not chased; `done` = a door user (KL denominators) |
| `gdm_waiting_opened` | — | waiting mode opened in the first session |
| `gdm_asklist_opened` | — | **KL-T1 denominator A** |
| `gdm_ask_parked` | `from: "list" \| "quick_add" \| "plan_differ"` | **KL-T1 numerator A** — a count, never text |
| `gdm_plan_entered` | `unit: GdmUnit; has_figure: boolean` | plans entered; unit chosen; **KL-T1 numerator B; KL-D1 numerator** |
| `gdm_meal_saved` | `occasion: GdmOccasion` | meals saved by day 7; **KL-T1 numerator B** |
| `gdm_summary_printed` | `before_appointment: "yes" \| "no" \| "no_date"` | summaries printed before an appointment date |

A clinical route firing on list text reuses the existing `clinical_route` event — no new one.

The row uses the ledger's eight-column shape:

```
| `<copy-id>` | GDM <surface> | Pending | Yes | `gdm-organiser` | <strings joined by " · ", plain text, no backticks> | FDA-GENERAL-WELLNESS, FTC-HEALTH-COMPLIANCE | PRD GDM v1.1 <§ and feature> (`<file that renders it>`). Filed Pending behind NEXT_PUBLIC_GDM_DOOR; class `gdm-organiser` does not exist in claims-boundary.md yet (PRD §8.1, S5) — this row cannot be Approved until the safety owner adds it. <one sentence: what the strings do NOT say>. |
```

4. Run `npm run test:pal -- gdm-copy` (families + ledger parity + flag parity) and `npm run contract` (cell count; a `Pending` row's unknown class is not an error). Expected: PASS, both.

**The status the executor writes is always `Pending`.** Never `Approved`.

// tests/unit/pal/gdm-landing.test.ts
import type { ReactElement, ReactNode } from "react";

import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";

import { GDM_COPY } from "../../../lib/pal/gdm/copy";

// The tests/unit/pal/get-the-app-page.test.ts pattern: call the server
// component, walk the element tree, no jsdom.
function collectText(node: ReactNode): string {
  if (node == null || typeof node === "boolean") return "";
  if (typeof node === "string" || typeof node === "number") return String(node);
  if (Array.isArray(node)) return node.map(collectText).join(" ");
  if (typeof node === "object" && "props" in node) {
    const props = (node as ReactElement).props as { children?: ReactNode; href?: string };
    return (props.href ? ` ${props.href} ` : "") + collectText(props.children);
  }
  return "";
}

async function renderLanding(): Promise<string> {
  vi.resetModules();
  const mod = await import("../../../app/gdm/page");
  return collectText(mod.default() as ReactElement);
}

describe("/gdm — the landing (PRD §3, §7.1)", () => {

```tsx
// app/gdm/layout.tsx — the frame only. No auth logic: each page does its own
// (a layout does not re-render on navigation and does not stop its children
// rendering — Next docs, 02-guides/authentication.md). The one check here is
// the BUILD-TIME flag: with the door dark the frame renders no copy at all, so
// a 404 under /gdm can never carry the working name before `gdm-organiser`
// exists — whether or not Next wraps a not-found boundary in this layout.
// Task L.2's smoke spec pins it rather than trusting that reasoning.
import type { ReactNode } from "react";

import { gdmDoorEnabled } from "../../lib/gdm-door-flag";
import { GDM_COPY } from "../../lib/pal/gdm/copy";

export default function GdmLayout({ children }: Readonly<{ children: ReactNode }>) {
  if (!gdmDoorEnabled("landing") && !gdmDoorEnabled("organiser")) return <>{children}</>;
  return (
    <>
      <a href="#gdm-content" className="app-skip">
        {GDM_COPY["gdm-door-name"].skip}
      </a>
      <main className="page-shell">
        <div className="page-frame" id="gdm-content" tabIndex={-1}>
          {/* G-38: no wordmark here. The landing's h1 IS the name; door pages get
              <GdmWordmark /> from (door)/layout.tsx, and start/privacy render it themselves. */}
          {children}
          <footer className="surface-card legal-card">
            <p>{GDM_COPY["gdm-disclaimer"].line}</p>
            {/* G-48 (owner): the maker, once, so the shared sign-in page is no surprise. */}
            <p>{GDM_COPY["gdm-maker"].line}</p>
          </footer>
        </div>
      </main>
    </>
  );
}
```

Read `node_modules/next/dist/docs/01-app/03-api-reference/03-file-conventions/01-metadata/` before writing any of this.

```tsx
// A function, not a constant (G-30): while the door is dark it returns {}, so a
// 404 under /gdm never carries the working name in its <title> or its preview.
export function generateMetadata(): Metadata {
  if (!gdmDoorEnabled("landing") && !gdmDoorEnabled("organiser")) return {};
  const { name, short } = GDM_COPY["gdm-door-name"];
  const line = GDM_COPY["gdm-landing-hero"].line;
  return {
    title: name,
    description: line,
    applicationName: name,
    manifest: GDM_ROUTES.manifest,
    appleWebApp: { capable: true, title: short, statusBarStyle: "default" },
    openGraph: { siteName: name, title: name, description: line },
    robots: { index: false, follow: false }
  };
}
```

  `opengraph-image.tsx` renders the working name **from the bank** on the page background with `ImageResponse` (no literal, no import from `lib/pal/labels`, which the root image uses and Task 0.5 forbids here) and returns `new Response(null, { status: 404 })` unless `gdmDoorEnabled("landing")`. The manifest is a **route handler, not a file in `public/`**: a static file would publish the working name while the door is dark. It 404s under the same condition as the layout and otherwise returns `name`, `short_name`, `start_url: GDM_ROUTES.start`, `scope: "/gdm"`, `display: "standalone"`, `theme_color: "#0d5f57"` and the existing `/icon.png` (the first door's mark stays on the installed icon; D7 forbids buying another). ```ts
// tests/smoke/gdm-door.spec.ts
import AxeBuilder from "@axe-core/playwright";
import { expect, test, type APIRequestContext } from "@playwright/test";

async function gdmSurfaceOn(request: APIRequestContext, surface: string): Promise<boolean> {
  const payload = await (await request.get("/api/health")).json();
  return payload?.gdmDoor?.[surface] === "on";
}

test.describe("GDM door — dark", () => {
  test.beforeEach(async ({ request }) => {
    test.skip(await gdmSurfaceOn(request, "landing"), "landing surface on in this build");
  });
  for (const path of ["/gdm", "/gdm/start", "/gdm/home", "/gdm/privacy"]) {
    test(`${path} is a 404 with the flag off — and the 404 carries no GDM string`, async ({ page }) => {
      expect((await page.goto(path))?.status()).toBe(404);
      // No user-visible GDM string before `gdm-organiser` exists: not even the
      // working name, not even on an error page rendered inside app/gdm/layout.tsx.
      const body = await page.locator("body").innerText();
      expect(body).not.toContain("Gestational Diabetes Organiser");
      expect(body).not.toMatch(/gestational/i);
    });
  }
});

test.describe("GDM door — landing", () => {
  test.beforeEach(async ({ request }) => {
    test.skip(!(await gdmSurfaceOn(request, "landing")), "landing surface off in this build");
  });

  test("leads with the working name, signs up through /signin, and is axe-clean", async ({ page }) => {
    await page.goto("/gdm");
    await expect(page.getByRole("heading", { level: 1 })).toHaveText("Gestational Diabetes Organiser");
    await expect(page.getByRole("link", { name: "Sign up" })).toHaveAttribute(
      "href",
      "/signin?callbackUrl=%2Fgdm%2Fstart"
    );
    expect((await new AxeBuilder({ page }).analyze()).violations).toEqual([]);
  });

  test("a signed-out visitor to the door is sent to sign in, never to the A1C form", async ({ page }) => {
    await page.goto("/gdm/start");
    await expect(page).toHaveURL(/\/signin\?callbackUrl=%2Fgdm%2Fstart/);
  });
});
```

#### PR-1 — The door shell: consent, A1C-free onboarding, erase and export

##### Task 1.1: Two tables, one migration

> **Review amendments (read before starting).**
> - **G-10 (HIGH, applied at the end of this task):** this migration must be applied to production **before PR-1 is merged**, and the reason is there in full.
> - **G-65, a trap:** `tests/unit/pal/privacy-stateful.test.ts:47-48` scans the **whole text** of `schema.ts` for a quoted `a1c` or `food`, not just column names. The four `kind` values below are fine. Never add a kind, a comment-free string or a check constraint containing `'food'` or `'a1c'`.
> - `createTestDb()` builds its database from `drizzle/*.sql`, not from `schema.ts` (verified). The steps below already generate the migration before the first passing run; do not reorder them.

**Files:**
- Modify: `lib/server/db/schema.ts`, `docs/privacy/data-flow.md` (stored-data allowlist `:35-48`)
- Create (generated, never hand-written): `drizzle/0020_gdm-door.sql`, `drizzle/meta/0020_snapshot.json`; `drizzle/meta/_journal.json` gains entry 20
- Test: `tests/unit/pal/gdm-schema.test.ts`; extend `tests/unit/server/db-schema.test.ts` (table list `:33-42`)

**Interfaces:**
- Produces: `schema.gdmProfiles` (`userId` PK, `consentedAt`, `appointmentCiphertext`, `createdAt`) — **a row's existence is the GDM profile flag**; `schema.gdmItems` (`id`, `userId`, `kind`, `bodyCiphertext`, `createdAt`, `updatedAt`).

One generic items table, not one table per list: one route, one erase line, one export key, row-level writes (no whole-document overwrite between two devices), and the kill lines get an exact cross-check (`count(distinct user_id) … group by kind`). `kind` is plaintext and coarse — the same posture as `profiles.a1c_band` beside `a1c_ciphertext`.

import { getTableColumns } from "drizzle-orm";
import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

import { schema } from "../../../lib/server/db";
import { createTestDb } from "../../helpers/test-db";

let testDb: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof createTestDb>>;
beforeAll(async () => {
  testDb = await createTestDb();
});
afterAll(async () => testDb.close());

describe("GDM tables (PRD §7.4: the profile flag and everything under it is health data)", () => {
  it("both tables are user-scoped, so the export denominator and the cascade see them", () => {
    expect(Object.keys(getTableColumns(schema.gdmProfiles))).toContain("userId");
    expect(Object.keys(getTableColumns(schema.gdmItems))).toContain("userId");
  });

  it("no column could hold plaintext health text, a lab value or a medication", () => {
    const columns = [
      ...Object.keys(getTableColumns(schema.gdmProfiles)),
      ...Object.keys(getTableColumns(schema.gdmItems))
    ];
    expect(columns.sort()).toEqual(

    );

  it("deleting the user cascades to both tables", async () => {
    const [user] = await testDb.db.insert(schema.users).values({ email: "gdm-schema@test.dev" }).returning({ id: schema.users.id });
    await testDb.db.insert(schema.gdmProfiles).values({ userId: user.id, consentedAt: new Date() });
    await testDb.db.insert(schema.gdmItems).values({ userId: user.id, kind: "ask", bodyCiphertext: "v1:x" });
    await testDb.raw.query("DELETE FROM users WHERE id = $1", [user.id]);
    for (const table of ["gdm_profiles", "gdm_items"]) {
      const result = await testDb.raw.query<{ n: number }>(`SELECT count(*)::int AS n FROM ${table} WHERE user_id = $1`, [user.id]);
      expect(result.rows[0].n, table).toBe(0);
    }

  it("kind is a closed set", async () => {
    const [user] = await testDb.db.insert(schema.users).values({ email: "gdm-kind@test.dev" }).returning({ id: schema.users.id });
    await expect(
      testDb.raw.query("INSERT INTO gdm_items (user_id, kind, body_ciphertext) VALUES ($1, 'reading', 'v1:x')", [user.id])
    ).rejects.toThrow();
  });
});

- [ ] **Step 3: Add the tables** to `lib/server/db/schema.ts` after `mealMemories`, using the file's own idioms (`uuid(...).defaultRandom()`, `timestamp(..., { withTimezone: true })`, `check(...)`, `index(...)`):

```ts
// ── GDM door (PRD GDM v1.1 §7.4) ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// A row here IS the GDM profile flag — health data, written only after explicit
// consent by POST /api/gdm/profile. No lab value is asked on this door, and no
// medication field exists (owner decision D2): do not add one.
export const gdmProfiles = pgTable("gdm_profiles", {
  userId: uuid("user_id")
    .primaryKey()
    .references(() => users.id, { onDelete: "cascade" }),
  consentedAt: timestamp("consented_at", { withTimezone: true }).notNull(),
  // Her appointment date ("YYYY-MM-DD"), encrypted. Null = not given.
  appointmentCiphertext: text("appointment_ciphertext"),
  createdAt: timestamp("created_at", { withTimezone: true }).notNull().defaultNow()
});

// Everything she keeps: parked asks, plan entries, meals, a photo of her sheet.
// `bodyCiphertext` is encryptField(JSON) validated per kind by lib/pal/gdm/items.ts.
// `kind` is plaintext and coarse (the a1c_band posture). `userId` is on the row
// itself — never reachable only through another GDM row — so the account export's
// schema-derived denominator and the explicit erase both see it.
export const gdmItems = pgTable(
  "gdm_items",
  {
    id: uuid("id").primaryKey().defaultRandom(),
    userId: uuid("user_id")
      .notNull()
      .references(() => users.id, { onDelete: "cascade" }),
    kind: text("kind", { enum: ["ask", "plan", "meal", "plan_photo"] }).notNull(),
    bodyCiphertext: text("body_ciphertext").notNull(),
    createdAt: timestamp("created_at", { withTimezone: true }).notNull().defaultNow(),
    updatedAt: timestamp("updated_at", { withTimezone: true }).notNull().defaultNow()
  },
  (table) => [
    check("gdm_items_kind_check", sql`${table.kind} IN ('ask','plan','meal','plan_photo')`),
    index("gdm_items_user_kind").on(table.userId, table.kind, table.createdAt)
  ]
);
```

| `gdm_items` | parked questions, plan entries, meals, a photo of her sheet | **ciphertext only** (`body_ciphertext`, JSON) |
| `gdm_items` | kind, timestamps | plaintext (coarse) |
```

**Release order (gate row "Migration `0020`") (G-10):** apply with `npm run db:migrate:production`, then `npm run db:governance:check`, **before PR-1 is merged** — the owner's step, not the executor's. The executor opens PR-1 and stops.

**Why this one dark PR is not safe to merge like the others.** `main` deploys on merge. `GET /api/account/export` and `DELETE /api/account/health-data` ignore the GDM flag on purpose (Task 1.3), so from PR-1 on they query `gdm_profiles` and `gdm_items` for *every* user. If that code reaches production before the tables do, export and erase return 500 for every existing prediabetes user, on the two routes that carry legal rights, while the door they belong to is dark and nobody is watching it. CI checks that the migration *file* matches the schema. Nothing checks that production has *applied* it.

##### Task 1.2: `/api/gdm/profile` — the consent path, with no A1C anywhere

> **Review amendments (read before starting).**
> - **G-22:** `consentedAt` is written **once**. In the `POST` below, `onConflictDoUpdate` sets only `appointmentCiphertext`; drop `consentedAt: now` from its `set`. That timestamp is the record that explicit consent was given, and a double-tap on "Agree and continue" must not move it. Add a case: POST twice, a second apart; `consented_at` is unchanged and the date is updated.
> - **G-16:** the closed → session → consent order is extracted in Task 2.1 as `lib/server/gdm-route.ts`. This route has no consent step of its own (it *is* the consent step), so it uses the helper's first two checks only.

`app/api/profile/route.ts` refuses any out-of-range A1C through `routeA1C`; a GDM user has no such A1C. This route is the path that never calls it: it takes no lab value, it takes no medication, and it stores nothing without consent.

**Files:**
- Create: `app/api/gdm/profile/route.ts`
- Modify: `lib/pal/gdm/copy.ts` + flag + ledger (row `gdm-consent-required`, surface `organiser`)
- Test: `tests/unit/pal/gdm-profile-route.test.ts`, `tests/unit/pal/gdm-a1c-free.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces: `createGdmProfileHandlers(deps?: { db?: () => Db; getSession?: () => Promise<SessionInfo> })` → `{ GET, POST, PATCH }`.
  - `POST { told: true, consent: boolean, appointmentDate?: "YYYY-MM-DD" | null }` (`.strict()`) → `200 { ok: true }` · `400` · `401` · `404` (door closed).
  - `GET` → `{ hasProfile: false }` or `{ hasProfile: true, appointmentDate: string | null }`.
  - `PATCH { appointmentDate: "YYYY-MM-DD" | null }` → `200` · `404` when no profile.

**Row filed:** `gdm-consent-required` — `line`: Keeping your plan, meals and questions needs your explicit health-data consent.

```ts
// tests/unit/pal/gdm-a1c-free.test.ts
import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

const ROOT = process.cwd();
const GDM_DIRS = ["app/gdm", "app/api/gdm", "components/gdm", "lib/pal/gdm", "lib/server/gdm-door.ts", "lib/client/gdm-api.ts"];

function sources(): Array<{ rel: string; text: string }> {
  const out: Array<{ rel: string; text: string }> = [];
  const visit = (rel: string) => {
    const abs = path.join(ROOT, rel);
    if (!fs.existsSync(abs)) return;
    if (fs.statSync(abs).isDirectory()) fs.readdirSync(abs).forEach((name) => visit(path.join(rel, name)));
    else if (/\.tsx?$/.test(rel)) out.push({ rel, text: fs.readFileSync(abs, "utf8") });
  };
  GDM_DIRS.forEach(visit);
  return out;
}

describe("the GDM door asks no A1C and has no medication field (PRD §7.1, §7.2, D2)", () => {
  it("found the door's sources", () => {
    expect(sources().length).toBeGreaterThan(3);
  });

  it("no GDM source names an A1C, in code or in comments", () => {
    expect(sources().filter(({ text }) => /a1c/i.test(text)).map(({ rel }) => rel)).toEqual([]);
  });

  it("no GDM source declares a medication-shaped field, input or schema key", () => {
    const FIELD = /\b(?:medication|medicine|meds|insulin|metformin|dose|dosage)\w*\s*[:=?]/i;
    expect(sources().filter(({ text }) => FIELD.test(text)).map(({ rel }) => rel)).toEqual([]);
  });

  it("no GDM surface links or hands off to a prediabetes-door route (PRD §7.1: /check is not reachable from this profile)", () => {
    const LINK = /["'`]\/(?:check|home|onboarding|welcome|journey|meals|learn|subscribe|trial)(?:["'`/?#])/;
    expect(sources().filter(({ text }) => LINK.test(text)).map(({ rel }) => rel)).toEqual([]);
  });
});
```

```ts
// app/api/gdm/profile/route.ts
import { eq } from "drizzle-orm";
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { z } from "zod";

import { gdmDoorEnabled, gdmDoorServerEnabled } from "../../../../lib/gdm-door-flag";
import { GDM_COPY } from "../../../../lib/pal/gdm/copy";
import { encryptField, safeDecrypt } from "../../../../lib/server/crypto";
import { getDb, schema, type Db } from "../../../../lib/server/db";
import { getSessionInfo, type SessionInfo } from "../../../../lib/server/session";

export const runtime = "nodejs";

// The GDM door's consent path. Deliberately NOT app/api/profile: that route
// requires a lab value and refuses one outside its range. This door asks for
// none, and asks nothing about medicine (owner decision D2) — both schemas are
// strict, so a field for either cannot be added by accident.
const IsoDate = z.string().regex(/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/);
const CreateSchema = z
  .object({ told: z.literal(true), consent: z.boolean(), appointmentDate: IsoDate.nullable().optional() })
  .strict();
const PatchSchema = z.object({ appointmentDate: IsoDate.nullable() }).strict();

type Deps = { db?: () => Db; getSession?: () => Promise<SessionInfo> };

const closed = () => !(gdmDoorServerEnabled() && gdmDoorEnabled("organiser"));
const notFound = () => NextResponse.json({ error: "Not found." }, { status: 404 });
const signIn = () => NextResponse.json({ error: "Sign in first." }, { status: 401 });
const invalid = () => NextResponse.json({ error: "Invalid request." }, { status: 400 });
const seal = (date: string | null | undefined) => (date ? encryptField(date) : null);
async function json(request: Request): Promise<unknown> {
  try {
    return await request.json();
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

export function createGdmProfileHandlers(deps: Deps = {}) {
  const db = deps.db ?? getDb;
  const getSession = deps.getSession ?? getSessionInfo;

  return {
    async GET() {
      if (closed()) return notFound();
      const session = await getSession();
      if (!session) return signIn();
      const [row] = await db()
        .select({ appointment: schema.gdmProfiles.appointmentCiphertext })
        .from(schema.gdmProfiles)
        .where(eq(schema.gdmProfiles.userId, session.userId));
      if (!row) return NextResponse.json({ hasProfile: false });
      return NextResponse.json({ hasProfile: true, appointmentDate: row.appointment ? safeDecrypt(row.appointment) : null });
    },

    async POST(request: Request) {
      if (closed()) return notFound();
      const session = await getSession();
      if (!session) return signIn();
      const parsed = CreateSchema.safeParse(await json(request));
      if (!parsed.success) return invalid();
      if (!parsed.data.consent) {
        // GDPR Art. 9: explicit consent is the lawful basis. No consent, no row.
        return NextResponse.json({ error: GDM_COPY["gdm-consent-required"].line }, { status: 400 });
      }
      const now = new Date();
      const appointmentCiphertext = seal(parsed.data.appointmentDate);
      await db()
        .insert(schema.gdmProfiles)
        .values({ userId: session.userId, consentedAt: now, appointmentCiphertext })
        // G-22: consentedAt is written ONCE. It is the record that explicit consent
        // was given; a double-tap on "Agree and continue" must not move it.
        .onConflictDoUpdate({ target: schema.gdmProfiles.userId, set: { appointmentCiphertext } });
      return NextResponse.json({ ok: true });
    },

    async PATCH(request: Request) {
      if (closed()) return notFound();
      const session = await getSession();
      if (!session) return signIn();
      const parsed = PatchSchema.safeParse(await json(request));
      if (!parsed.success) return invalid();
      const updated = await db()
        .update(schema.gdmProfiles)
        .set({ appointmentCiphertext: seal(parsed.data.appointmentDate) })
        .where(eq(schema.gdmProfiles.userId, session.userId))
        .returning({ userId: schema.gdmProfiles.userId });
      return updated.length === 0 ? notFound() : NextResponse.json({ ok: true });
    }
  };
}

const handlers = createGdmProfileHandlers();
export const GET = handlers.GET;
export const POST = handlers.POST;
export const PATCH = handlers.PATCH;
```

`safeDecrypt` returns the `"(unreadable entry)"` placeholder on a key problem; the date field then shows that string, which Task 4.1's `appointmentPhrase` treats as "no date".

##### Task 1.4: The door guard, the onboarding, Your data, and the privacy notice

> **Review amendments (read before starting).** This task builds the door's first interactive screens, and the plan specified what they say far more fully than what they do.
> - **G-27 (HIGH, applied below):** "back" went forward from the date step, and two steps had no string for a back control.
> - **G-35:** the consent button is **enabled**. A disabled button takes no focus and explains nothing. Pressing it with the box unticked shows `gdm-consent-required.line` (already in the bank) in `role="alert"`, tied to the checkbox with `aria-describedby`. The box is still unticked by default, and the server still refuses an unconsented POST.
> - **G-47:** on the consent step the notice opens **in place**: a native `<details>` whose `<summary>` is the existing `notice` string and whose body is the four `gdm-privacy-notice` paragraphs. Following a link to `/gdm/privacy` would throw away the date she just typed. The full page stays linked from the footer.
> - **G-34 (owner decision), the nav:** row one is the wordmark (it is the Home link) and "Add a question" as the door's one filled action; row two is the working surfaces (plan, meals, questions, summary, as each PR adds them) in one wrapping `.chip-row` (`DESIGN.md` §10); **Your data and the privacy notice sit in the frame's footer**, on every page. "Add a question" is not rendered on the questions page. So `NAV` starts empty here rather than as `[home, data]`. Seven entries in one `<nav>` wrap to three rows at 375 px.
> - **G-28:** `app/gdm/(door)/layout.tsx` takes the same build-flag check as the outer layout: with `organiser` off it returns `<>{children}</>` and renders no nav.
> - **G-26, G-36:** every submit disables itself while in flight, and each page has one `aria-live="polite"` region. New row `gdm-status` (surface `organiser`) — `saving`: Saving · `saved`: Saved. · `removed`: Removed. · `loading`: Loading. A failed save leaves the form exactly as she filled it.
> - **G-14:** `gdmFetch` sends a **401** to `GDM_ROUTES.signup` instead of returning it; "try again in a moment" can never work for an expired session. Every text input carries a `maxLength` equal to its zod bound.
> - **G-15:** create `app/gdm/error.tsx` (a client component; its button calls `reset()`) and row `gdm-load-failed` — `line`: That did not load just now. · `retry`: Try again. No error boundary exists anywhere under `app/` today, so a database hiccup would otherwise show an anxious user Next's raw error page.
> - **G-05:** `gdm-data-controls.eraseWarn` must say what the shared route really erases. Draft: "This erases all the health data in your account, including everything you have kept here. It cannot be undone." `DELETE /api/account/health-data` covers the first door's data too.
> - **G-39:** on a step change, focus moves to the new step's `h1` (`tabIndex={-1}`). On either two-step confirm below, focus moves onto the confirm control.
> - **G-77: Your data, in this order and weight.** Download my data · Sign out · then, visibly separated and never in the filled accent, Erase my health data and Delete my account. As first listed the two destructive actions sat side by side at equal weight with near-identical labels, and the everyday one, Sign out, came last.

- Create: `lib/server/gdm-door.ts`, `lib/pal/gdm/onboarding.ts`, `lib/client/gdm-api.ts`, `components/gdm/onboarding.tsx`, `components/gdm/nav.tsx`, `components/gdm/data-controls.tsx`, `app/gdm/(door)/layout.tsx`, `app/gdm/(door)/home/page.tsx`, `app/gdm/(door)/data/page.tsx`, `app/gdm/privacy/page.tsx`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces:
  - `gdmDoorState(deps?): Promise<{ kind: "closed" } | { kind: "signed_out" } | { kind: "no_profile"; userId: string } | { kind: "open"; userId: string; appointmentDate: string | null }>` and `requireGdmDoor(): Promise<{ userId: string; appointmentDate: string | null }>` — **every page under `app/gdm/(door)/` calls `requireGdmDoor()` first.**
  - `type GdmStep = "told" | "not_told" | "date" | "consent"`; `nextGdmStep(step, answer: "yes" | "not_yet" | "continue" | "skip" | "back"): GdmStep | "submit"`.
  - `lib/client/gdm-api.ts`: `gdmFetch<T>(path, init?): Promise<{ ok: true; data: T } | { ok: false; status: number; error: string }>` — one wrapper, JSON in and out, never throws; and `clearGdmDeviceKeys(storages?: Array<Pick<Storage, "length" | "key" | "removeItem">>): void` — removes every `pal.gdm.*` key from `localStorage` and `sessionStorage` (try/catch-wrapped; storages injectable for the test).

**Rows filed (surface `organiser`):**

| Copy ID | Named strings |
|---|---|
| `gdm-nav` | `home`: Home · `plan`: My plan · `meals`: My meals · `asks`: My questions · `summary`: Summary · `data`: Your data · `add`: Add a question · `label`: Organiser |
| `gdm-onboarding-told` | `ask`: Have you been told you have gestational diabetes? · `yes`: Yes · `notYet`: Not yet |
| `gdm-onboarding-not-told` | `line`: This organiser is built for people who have been told they have gestational diabetes. For questions about testing, your care team is the place to ask. · `back`: Back |
| `gdm-onboarding-date` | `ask`: When is your appointment? · `hint`: Optional. It stays with your notes. · `next`: Continue · `skip`: Skip · `back`: Back |
| `gdm-onboarding-consent` | `what`: What you keep here is health data. It is stored encrypted, only for you, and you can erase it at any time from Your data. · `agree`: I agree to Gestational Diabetes Organiser storing the health data I enter. · `go`: Agree and continue · `notice`: How your data is handled · `back`: Back |
| `gdm-save-failed` | `line`: That did not save just now. Try again in a moment. |
| `gdm-data-controls` | `title`: Your data · `download`: Download my data · `erase`: Erase my health data · `eraseWarn`: This erases everything you have kept here. It cannot be undone. · `eraseGo`: Erase · `deleteAccount`: Delete my account · `signOut`: Sign out · `cancel`: Cancel |
| `gdm-privacy-notice` | `title`: How your data is handled · `what`: What this organiser stores: that you have signed up here, your appointment date if you add one, and the plan, meals and questions you type or photograph. · `how`: How it is stored: encrypted, tied to your account, and used only to show it back to you. · `never`: What is never done with it: your words are not sent to an analytics service, and they are not used to build a profile of you. · `choices`: Your choices: download it or erase it at any time from Your data. Erasing removes it from our database. |

**(G-27)** The `back` string on `gdm-onboarding-date` and on `gdm-onboarding-consent` was added by the review: every step after the first can go back, and a control needs a string because components carry no literal. The string is the one word `Back`.

The not-told line says what PRD §7.2 allows and nothing more: who the door is for, and that her care team is the place for questions about testing. "Nothing about tests, thresholds or what a result means."
